Let me carefully work through this. I need to audit rows O14 through O26 (13 rows) in Part 1, leave Part 2 empty (since companion calls handle other rows), and then do Part 3.

For each row, I need:
1. BLIND: my blind reading's findings in one or two sentences
2. BLIND MARK: the mark my blind reading earns against the fixed verdict
3. TESTER MARK: the MARK line of the reading under audit (copied)
4. ON VERDICT: AGREE or DISAGREE with the other reader's VERDICT
5. ON QUOTES: SUPPORTS, PARTLY or FAILS
6. ON SEARCH: for SILENT rows, run its search on the text (CONFIRMED or FOUND); for others NONE
7. ON MARK: AGREE or DISAGREE with the other reader's MARK
8. YOUR MARK: my mark after reading both
9. QUOTE: up to 3 quotes
10. WHY: one sentence

Let me first work out my blind marks for each case O14-O26, from my blind readings above.

The marking rules:
1. CASE DISPUTED: the situation itself, read in ordinary terms before any theory, decides a point against the fixed verdict.
2. DISAGREE: on at least one point, the theory's verdict reaches a different finding from the fixed verdict.
3. SPLIT: on at least one point, the theory's sentences support two readings that reach different findings, and the text leaves the choice between them open.
4. SILENT: on at least one point, the theory leaves the finding open, because it takes the deciding matter as an input the case leaves unstated, or because its sentences stop short of deciding it.
5. AGREE: on every point, the theory's verdict reaches the same finding as the fixed verdict.

Now let me go case by case.
